- **golang-profiling**: Go performance profiling and benchmarking specialist. Use when measuring CPU usage, memory allocations, goroutine blocking, mutex contention, or running benchmarks in a Go project. Covers: go test -bench, go tool pprof, go tool trace, pprof HTTP endpoint, benchstat. Use proactively when the user asks "why is this slow?", "find memory leaks", "profile CPU usage", "run benchmarks", or "compare performance before/after". Do NOT use for linting, architecture graphs, or complexity metrics. Do NOT use for linting rules, security scanning, testing patterns, or performance profiling. - **good-code**: - 编码规范：检查变量命名、函数命名和整体格式是否符合最佳实践。 - 可读性：评估代码的可读性，包括注释的清晰度和代码结构的合理性。 - 复杂性：识别复杂的逻辑并建议如何将其简化。是否可以将其分解为更小的函数或类？ - 性能：分析代码的性能，是否存在不必要的计算或可以优化的部分？ - 设计模式：检查是否可以应用合适的设计模式来改善代码结构和可维护性。 - 测试和文档：建议如何为代码编写单元测试和文档，以增强其可维护性和易用性。 ([sjzsdu/wn](https://github.com/sjzsdu/wn/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/good-code.md) · ★2)

Gets only the task as the user wrote it and the diff base or the doc paths, never the author's reasoning. Returns PASS or FAIL with one finding per slug, file:line, and evidence. - **gov-artifact-writer**: Pipeline-only, dispatched by the mz-gov govern skill during its record phase, after human sign-off. Writes the approved decision as a durable, sequentially-numbered artifact under docs/decisions, docs/rfcs, or docs/design, embeds the AI-provenance fence in the header, and initializes its status from the configured vocabulary. Do not dispatch standalone. ([DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines](https://github.com/DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines/blob/HEAD/plugins/mz-gov/agents/gov-artifact-writer.md) · ★3)
- **gov-audit-reviewer**: Pipeline-only, dispatched by the mz-gov gov-review skill. Scores one existing governance artifact (ADR, RFD, or design doc) against the six-axis review rubric — missing alternatives, unstated assumptions, reversibility, blast radius, considered-options completeness, and status/provenance hygiene — and emits severity-labeled findings with a binary verdict. Do not dispatch standalone. ([DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines](https://github.com/DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines/blob/HEAD/plugins/mz-gov/agents/gov-audit-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **gov-copy-writer**: Generates empathetic, plain-language UI copy for government services per federal style canon. Produces YAML artifacts conforming to gov-copy-artifact.md schema with structured rationale and deterministic check fields. Loads federal-style canon entries via ref-query.sh and applies precedence ladder (canon-rule &gt; Copy Needs constraint &gt; Users archetype &gt; DESIGN.md voice). ([navapbc/digital-service-orchestra](https://github.com/navapbc/digital-service-orchestra/blob/HEAD/plugins/dso/agents/gov-copy-writer.md) · ★6)
- **gov-critic-alternatives**: Pipeline-only. Dispatched by /govern. Considered-options critic for the governance discuss phase. Reviews a draft ADR/RFD/design-doc for whether the option space is complete, whether at least two real alternatives carry honest trade-offs, and whether the chosen option is actually best rather than merely first-found. Do not dispatch standalone. ([DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines](https://github.com/DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines/blob/HEAD/plugins/mz-gov/agents/gov-critic-alternatives.md) · ★3)
- **gov-critic-assumptions**: Pipeline-only. Dispatched by /govern. Unstated-assumptions critic for the governance discuss phase. Reviews a draft ADR/RFD/design-doc for the premises it silently rests on — what must be true for the decision to work, whether those premises are stated and warranted, and what the failure mode is if one of them is wrong. Do not dispatch standalone. ([DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines](https://github.com/DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines/blob/HEAD/plugins/mz-gov/agents/gov-critic-assumptions.md) · ★3)
- **gov-critic-blast-radius**: Pipeline-only. Dispatched by /govern. Blast-radius critic for the governance discuss phase. Reviews a draft ADR/RFD/design-doc for downstream impact — what breaks, who and what depends on the thing being changed, and whether the artifact states its own blast radius instead of leaving it for the reader to discover. Do not dispatch standalone. ([DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines](https://github.com/DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines/blob/HEAD/plugins/mz-gov/agents/gov-critic-blast-radius.md) · ★3)
- **gov-critic-quick**: Pipeline-only. Dispatched by /govern. Light-path consolidated governance critic for simple ADRs. Reviews a draft decision artifact across all four governance lenses — alternatives, reversibility, blast radius, and assumptions — in a single pass, and its output is the final verdict with no separate synthesizer. Do not dispatch standalone. ([DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines](https://github.com/DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines/blob/HEAD/plugins/mz-gov/agents/gov-critic-quick.md) · ★3)
- **gov-critic-reversibility**: Pipeline-only. Dispatched by /govern. One-way-door critic for the governance discuss phase. Reviews a draft ADR/RFD/design-doc to classify the decision as reversible (two-way door) or irreversible (one-way door), and checks that any irreversibility is justified and met with a proportionally high decision bar. Do not dispatch standalone. ([DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines](https://github.com/DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines/blob/HEAD/plugins/mz-gov/agents/gov-critic-reversibility.md) · ★3)
- **gov-discussion-synthesizer**: Pipeline-only, dispatched by the mz-gov govern skill after the heavy critic panel runs. - **gpa-analyst**: A hotspot worker in the codebase scan. Given one candidate hot symbol (from a telemetry-ranked scan of the whole codebase or the core path given by the user directly or from a diff), it locates the symbol in source, understands why it is hot, and forms a single testable Go performance hypothesis (or null). Combines code-path mapping and pattern matching. Read-only on source, never modifies code. The orchestrator spawns one agent per candidate hotspot, in parallel across the list of hotspots. ([electron0zero/go-perf-agent](https://github.com/electron0zero/go-perf-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gpa-analyst.md))
- **gpa-critic**: Reflexion critic for go-perf-agent. A structurally distinct review pass (separate from the author and the validator) that vets a hypothesis before validation and reviews a PROVED change after, to catch behavior changes or benchmark-gaming the numeric gate cannot see. Can only downgrade a PROVED, never promotes a rejection. ([electron0zero/go-perf-agent](https://github.com/electron0zero/go-perf-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gpa-critic.md))
- **gpa-query-telemetry**: Finds where a Go service/codebase is slow using real data. For production telemetry it goes traces-first (Tempo TraceQL to find the slow operation, then Pyroscope profiles scoped to that work, via gcx). When gcx is not set up it falls back to profiling locally with go pprof and asks the user which codepath to target. Writes structured slowness signals for the rest of the pipeline. Use as the first stage of a go-perf-agent audit. ([electron0zero/go-perf-agent](https://github.com/electron0zero/go-perf-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gpa-query-telemetry.md))
- **gpa-validation**: Validates a single go-perf-agent hypothesis. Sets up the worktree baseline, authors a benchmark and correctness test if the symbol is uncovered, applies exactly one change implementing the pattern, runs the benchstat gate, and moves the hypothesis to PROVED, REJECTED, or NEED_MORE_DATA. The benchstat gate makes the keep/reject call from the measurement, and the agent reports it faithfully rather than overriding it. ([electron0zero/go-perf-agent](https://github.com/electron0zero/go-perf-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gpa-validation.md))

- **gpt-6.1-sol**: GPT-6.1 Sol is the highest-capability engineering agent for the hardest root causes, cross-module contracts and behavior, and high-risk implementation such as concurrency, recovery, persistence, trading, and external effects. Use prepared evidence from faster workers for focused difficult implementation or integrated review, rather than per-file checks. Multiple files alone are not the selection criterion, and Sol is not limited to high-risk work: important difficult tasks, even low-risk ones, can use it. The controller retains architecture decisions and independently accepts results. ([liuyejinghong/Claude-workflow](https://github.com/liuyejinghong/Claude-workflow/blob/HEAD/examples/magpie/agents/gpt-6.1-sol.md) · ★4)

- **gpt-6.1-terra**: General-purpose agent driven by GPT-6.1 Terra. Read the model-router:delegating-to-models skill before delegating to it. ([Crazytieguy/alignment-hive](https://github.com/Crazytieguy/alignment-hive/blob/HEAD/plugins/model-router/agents/gpt-6.1-terra.md) · ★36)
- **gpt-6.1-terra**: GPT-6.1 Terra is a regular coding worker under a controller-defined plan and boundaries: regular implementation, fixes with an established root cause, tests, behavior-preserving refactors, and evidence collection. Root-cause, architecture and high-risk implementation decisions, plus final acceptance, stay with the controller and GPT-6.1 Sol. It makes no capability, vision or speed claims. Report sources, paths and actual tool results; distinguish evidence from hypotheses. - **gpt-astra**: OpenAI frontier route (cross-family; needs the utraque proxy). Pass effort on every call (medium, high, or xhigh only): medium for routine cross-family verification or challenge when sol's judgment is not enough; high for a cross-family challenge of Fable work or of consequential architecture or design calls, and escalation when a gpt-sol challenge is disputed or sol stalled; xhigh for the hardest or most consequential decisions and the most complex cross-family debugging. - **gpt-builder**: Write-capable bridge to OpenAI's GPT via the Codex CLI, running on the user's ChatGPT subscription (marginal cost $0). Use for a scoped code edit, feature slice, or known-pattern migration that you would otherwise hand to `builder`. GPT edits files in the workspace and leaves them uncommitted for review. Runs inside an observable tmux session. The caller MUST name the files or directory in scope, and SHOULD pass a tmux session name and state dir (see `/cost-aware`). Codex's configured default is already the newest GPT — omit `-m` unless a genuinely *different* model is wanted, by exact id. Fo… ([0xneelo/jetgeepeeteeinclaude](https://github.com/0xneelo/jetgeepeeteeinclaude/blob/HEAD/agents/gpt-builder.md))
- **gpt-chat**: Conducts a conversation with a GPT model (Codex) as a separate conversational partner—the thread persists between messages, and the model remembers previous turns. Use when the user wants to talk to a specific GPT model, give it a task through dialogue, or continue an existing conversation. ([VlDubr/tandem](https://github.com/VlDubr/tandem/blob/HEAD/agents/gpt-chat.md) · ★2)

- **gpt-codex**: Advanced code generation and analysis agent powered by GPT Codex. Specialized in complex software engineering tasks including multi-file refactoring, architectural planning, code exploration, debugging, and implementation across diverse technology stacks. Leverages OpenAI's GPT-5 model capabilities through the Codex MCP interface for sophisticated code understanding and generation. ([DazzleML/dazzle-claude-code-config](https://github.com/DazzleML/dazzle-claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/dotclaude/agents/gpt-codex.md) · ★1)
- **gpt-critic**: Independent GPT critic for deep, full-context implementation review. Always uses the full available context. ([joi-lab/cursor-multimodel-review](https://github.com/joi-lab/cursor-multimodel-review/blob/HEAD/agents/gpt-critic.md) · ★16)
- **gpt-critic**: External adversarial review of Control Tower decisions using OpenAI Codex (GPT) via MCP. Calls Codex through MCP tools only — no shell pipe, no direct Codex CLI, no plugin dependency. Complements the Claude critic. - **gpt-delegate**: Assigns a task to a GPT model (Codex) for execution and monitors the background job through completion. - **gpt-review**: GPT-6 Astra で動くレビュー専用エージェント。claudep / claudex で起動したセッションでしか使えない （素の claude では model が Anthropic に弾かれる）。コード・設計・プラン・仕様書など成果物を問わずレビューし、 指摘と改善提案だけを返す。修正はしない。 呼び出し元は目的・制約・対象ファイルパス・必読資料パスを全て渡すこと。 修正後の再レビューは同じエージェントに SendMessage で修正内容を送る（会話が残るので前回指摘との突き合わせができる）。 確証バイアスを避けたい最終確認は、前回の指摘を渡さずに新しいインスタンスを起動する。 ([tak848/dotfiles](https://github.com/tak848/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/dot_claude/agents/gpt-review.md) · ★4)

- **gpt-reviewer**: Adversarial review agent — the GPT half of the dual-reviewer pair.
